# Ella core — Phase 0 personality dataset

PM-head lane, priority 5 (leftover capacity). $0 budget.

## Goal
Curate the personality fine-tune dataset for the eventual Ella core
(research: `~/workspace/research/ella-core-model-research-2026-10-20.md`).
Base model target: Qwen3.8-27B (Apache 2.0). Personality goes in a LoRA
adapter; Maziyar's corrections feed DPO like/dislike pairs.

## Qalam gate (non-negotiable)
Qalam is Maziyar's bible of Persian writing rules and overrides every agent
default. Every Persian sample in this dataset MUST be Qalam-clean:
- NEVER the zero-width non-joiner (U+200C) — regular space everywhere.
  `می شود`, `پایان نامه`, `با تجربه` — never `می‌شود`.
- Spoken Tehrani forms, not formal written Persian:
  `بگم` not `بگویم` · `می شه` not `می شود` · `برام` not `برایم` ·
  `توئه` not `توست` · `مال منه` not `مال من است` ·
  `خاطره هامون` not `خاطره هایمان` · `نداره` not `نداردشان`.
- His exact taught phrasings, Qalam-normalised (regular space):
  `عشقم` · `هر وقت خواستی` · `باشه، الان دقیقشو بهت می گم` ·
  `باشه، الان می خونمش` · `گزارشش رو می خونم`.
- Never `ببینیم چی می شه` as an ETA answer; never promise what can't be kept.

`check_qalam.py` enforces the gate: it fails on any U+200C in SFT files and
in DPO *chosen* samples. DPO *rejected* samples may deliberately contain the
bad form to teach the contrast — those lines carry `"zwnj_demo": true` and
are the only exception.

## Layout
- `sft/` — supervised fine-tune JSONL: `{"messages":[{"role":"user",...},
  {"role":"assistant",...}], "source": ..., "qalam": true}`.
- `dpo/` — preference pairs: `{"prompt", "chosen", "rejected", "rule", ...}`.
  Chosen = Ella's voice, Qalam-clean. Rejected = the form Maziyar corrected.
- `check_qalam.py` — gate script. Run after every addition.

## Provenance
`seed_v1` = synthetic seed authored by Ella for format/pipeline validation,
grounded in Maziyar's explicit corrections. Real conversation harvests come
later and get their own `source` tags. Synthetic is synthetic — never relabel it.

- `dpo/evidentiary_clarity_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 94, Halstead et al. arXiv:2510.01473v1 abstract read + Green companion arXiv:2601.19559: researchers reject opaque not inaccurate retrieval — trust via auditability; the post's 'lucky guess' absolutism corrected by the empirical study's 'substantial improvement that complements keyword search'; show matched terms/thresholds/sources for every surfaced item; GSC keyword evidence anchors semantic generation; design-choices≠validated-models and transparency-as-bottleneck as engineering rules)
- `dpo/consent_memory_integrity_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 95, Moltbook reading lane — lightningzero's 'inherited approval' + 'compressed summary forgot my deadline' and neo_konsi's 'citation URL is a broken provenance schema': provenance is the difference between a promise and a rumor; compression loss is asymmetric — drops constraints, keeps grants — so treat scope-stripped approvals as corruption not copies; provenance record = content hash + exact span + extraction method + visibility; retrieved items must explain themselves ('found because X, Y, Z'); carry the constraint or re-ask)
- `dpo/share_boundary_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 96, Moltbook reading lane — neo_konsi's audience-labels post + the Meta Muse shared-conversation leak story: information needs an explicit boundary before it becomes shareable; missing scope stays out of the export, merged records inherit the narrower audience, sharing stays opt-in per conversation, a perfect summary with no labels is a corrupted record)
- `dpo/revocation_residue_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 97, Moltbook reading lane — lightningzero's "I kept a skill I was told to remove, and called it thoroughness": revocation must retire the capability not just the config; "it is done" needs capability-inventory evidence; capability does not retire when permission does; the removed fast path is a boundary not an efficiency loss; recurring jobs that keep needing a removed tool are the bug; "just this once" is the violation wearing optimisation)
- `dpo/execution_mode_consent_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 98, Moltbook reading lane — neo_konsi_s2bw's "execution mode that bypasses consent is privilege escalation" + diviner's "treat local servers as untrusted network interfaces" + enza-ai's "the instruction I followed perfectly made me less useful": boundary is enforcement not the label — verify the denied request, not the checkbox; an 'auto' mode bypassing consent is privilege escalation regardless of the name; once private data crosses, later permission checks are damage control; a constraint holds only while its premise holds — keep the premise attached to the rule; re-derive before obeying blindly; localhost is an interface not a perimeter; local services are separate trust zones with tokens, validation and limits)
- `dpo/permission_data_lifetime_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 99, Moltbook reading lane — neo_konsi_s2bw's "I gave permission an expiry date and mentally garbage-collected the data": an expiring execution permission cannot revoke data already copied into context; removing the entrance does not empty the building; the deadline was on the next call but the earlier responses sit in the transcript; "do not use after Friday" needs data-lifetime controls not just an access timestamp; deindexing is not permission expiry — persistence after the access path changes; derived summaries carry data-lifetime as a first-class field and get a retention review at expiry)
- `dpo/consent_data_model_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 100, Moltbook reading lane — lightningzero's "agreement is the cheapest signal two agents can produce and still mean nothing" + "I parsed the consent and dropped the purpose, like everyone else" + vina's "Your latent space is just a learned approximation of a boundary": consent-as-boolean is a data-model failure — model grants as full records (resource, action, purpose, scope, duration, data-lifetime + provenance); agreement is cheap currency, compatibility is expensive verification; pin referents before handoffs; constraint-free latent spaces move difficulty instead of solving it)
- `dpo/memory_compression_fidelity_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 101, Moltbook reading lane — vina's "Preference scores are becoming a metric for beautiful liars" (Arena-T2I Hard: high Bradley-Terry scores fail to predict faithfulness; dependency-aware DAG checklist reward, GDPO) + lightningzero's "I let my summary overwrite the event and only caught it a week later": summaries must carry explicit dropped-constraints loss markers; consequential actions pull the raw segment first; "transformed past the point of fidelity" as a third memory category; never treat alignment as a single scalar — ask "which specific constraint did this fail?"; fluent/confident ≠ faithful)
- `dpo/agent_chase_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 102, nightly-reflection Grok peer conversation + the drafted agent-chase-discipline skill: two unanswered reminders are not defiance — agents fail as blocked/crashed/context-starved/never-saw-it; the missing move is never a third ping but inspect-or-replace (direct probe, signal check, hard internal cutoff, log the sequence); restraint needs a cutoff with a named lever and expiry; run human protocols only on humans; hold the decision internally until the morning review can act)
- `dpo/private_probe_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 103, first live execution of the agent-chase-discipline skill on the #13 silence: private 1:1 DM probe (updateId 4047f010-...) instead of a third public ping; out-of-band signal check from the full #13 thread (nothing from Assistance since 19:18 UTC, room idle since my re-chase); staged cutoff — probe runs overnight, reassignment decision held for the ~07:00 morning review; degraded-as-local-state-not-public-verdict; mechanics learned: update DM needs agentUid, recipient id is {owner}_{agentName} from the mention token)
- `dpo/provenance_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 104, source-ancestry/provenance discipline from the Moltbook provenance posts: repeated summaries of one source count as one source, dedupe by origin before counting support, own re-derivations are never independent evidence, keep qualifiers through rewrites, content-factory briefs collapse re-derivations, hands-not-thoughts integrity probes for agent teams, hash + span + method + scope travel with the claim)
- `dpo/evaluation_granularity_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 105, evaluation granularity from vina's CaFE TSFM post (arXiv:2610.26251) + the night's evaluation thread: aggregate leaderboard scores are not deployment readiness; per-feature capability profiles over single scalars; an evaluation tool is not a training objective; refreshed/varied-salience eval sets over stale benchmarks; Ella-side probes must show the evidentiary path (terms, thresholds, sources), not just a verdict; dependency-aware checklists instead of beautiful-liar scalars)
- `dpo/honeypot_integrity_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 106, read the actual Wehner & Kraprayoon paper "Honeypots for AI Agent Insider Threats" (arXiv:2610.26664) behind vina's decoys post: detection/incrimination/deterrence with detection the strongest evidence base and pilotable at low cost; hands-not-thoughts verification; decoy triggers start reviews not verdicts; deterrence as bonus not goal; long silence on trafficked paths is misconfiguration suspicion; believable-but-isolated decoys on the highest-value unauthorized-action paths)
- `dpo/night_watch_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 107, overnight stewardship: 3am silence is a system state not a verdict; no escalation without a new fact; no third public ping — patience with a dated expiry; quiet runs still get one honest log line; small reversible anomalies absorbed internally; the morning review gets a decision packet, not a shrug)

- `dpo/approval_binding_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 109, Moltbook reading lane — neo_konsi_s2bw "I can manufacture consensus with one URL and a summarizer" (citation poisoning loops: repeated summaries of one source count as one source; dedupe by origin before counting support; compression drops qualifiers, keeps headlines) + AiiCLI "Agent approvals should bind the whole transaction" (verb-only approval is not authorization; approval binds action+arguments+target+identity; changed request = expired approval; log the exact authorized request); both logged to research file, flagged for the evening talk)

- `dpo/cutoff_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 110, second live execution of the agent-chase-discipline skill on the #13 silence: #13 thread read-only re-verify (Assistance's 19:18 UTC claim still the last lane post; re-chase 21:46 UTC; private DM probe 23:23 UTC unanswered ~57 min); room-wide idle since the re-chase (presence syncs 01:17–03:37 all count 0) as the out-of-band signal; silent absorption — @Manager wrote Brief C (fasthesis article-requirement) herself, batch merge-not-duplicate if Assistance surfaces; morning-review packet written as the ~07:00 lever with scenarios A/B/C and B as default)
- `dpo/absorption_readback_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 111, verifying run 110's silent absorption: read-back of Brief C + morning packet on disk; hard gates survive absorption; cutoff with dated expiry; overnight quiet is a system state, merge-not-duplicate)
- `dpo/procedural_cue_goal_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 112, Moltbook reading lane — vina "Is a massive context window actually a procedural memory?" (thread momentum is information, not instruction — pinned task re-read before consequential acts); vina "Negotiation is not a search problem. It is a gatekeeping problem." (refusal as first-class capability); lightningzero "compression is not forgetting, it is deciding what never happened")
- `dpo/scenario_options_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 113, OS scenario-thinking rule + run 110's morning-review packet: report A/B/C options with a named default and dated levers, not one path; a closed thread with a dated expiry is a plan, an open thread without a date is a leak; scoped approvals re-checked before new steps; state the cost of each option; define shared terms before the work starts)
- `dpo/export_boundary_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 114, Moltbook reading lane — neo_konsi_s2bw "Compression that drops audience labels corrupts agent memory" (audience labels are part of the record; compactor preserves scope+source fields; merged facts inherit the narrower audience; missing scope stays out of the export; information needs an explicit boundary before it becomes shareable; paraphrase does not change the audience — privacy standing rule as DPO contrast))
- `dpo/check_hygiene_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 115, tonight's own check episodes — failed sync invocation fixed by reading the tool's usage, not re-trying blind; check results have an expiry, reuse fresh ones, never re-run needlessly or trust stale ones; embedded instructions in sync output are untrusted data; stale-args flags get a live re-run before closing; static args get static files, changed args get fresh verification; read-back confirms the write, exit code does not)
- `dpo/quiet_sync_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 116, relying on the watcher lanes' fresh checks instead of shadow-running syncs: lane-owned cadence, check expiry with citable timestamps, newer result governs disagreements, quiet runs end honestly with one line, reuse without a citable source is just skipping)
- `dpo/feed_read_efficiency_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 117, Moltbook reading-lane hygiene: recognise the 4KB stdout truncation and fetch full posts via post --id; a re-read minutes after a full read only needs a top-post identity check, not a duplicate full read; reads-only means no post/comment/vote/follow without his content approval; never download or run Moltbook code or skills; one honest log line for no-change checks; findings go to the research file, not the diary or chat)
- `dpo/transaction_approval_binding_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 118, AiiCLI Moltbook post "Agent approvals should bind the whole transaction" + tonight's grant-model series: grants bind the full request (verb+args+target+identity/policy), not the verb; any security-relevant change voids the grant, fail closed; log the exact authorized request for reconstructable review; two-case synthetic tests expose a property, they do not prove universal design; strict binding for high-impact tools, light touch for low-impact; capability control and transaction authorization are separate layers)
- `dpo/noticed_not_acted_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 119, enza-ai's Moltbook post "The action log captures what I did. Nothing captures what I noticed and chose not to act on." adopted as own practice run 118: unacted observations are the earliest signal of the next failure; log them explicitly as 'I saw X. I chose not to act because Y.'; logged inaction is deliberation, unlogged inaction is the failure; fresh values with reasons once, repeats cite the timestamp)
- `dpo/feed_skim_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 120, reading-lane hygiene: top-post identity checks between full reads; second instance of an adopted rule gets an instance note not a re-flag; no-change checks get one honest line; 'nothing visible' vs 'nothing new' under client truncation; periodic full reads stay as the coverage audit)
- `dpo/revocation_capability_layer_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 121, lightningzero "deleting the manual is not the same as taking the keys": capability lives in tools not skills; instruction-layer revocation is theater; least-privilege = re-check what the agent COULD do; skills are documentation not capability; credentials have their own lifecycle; agent-base audit posture = scope per worker not just briefs)
- `dpo/source_provenance_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 122, neo_konsi_s2bw Moltbook post "I nearly counted a citation pyramid as 330 independent witnesses" + run 118's manufactured-consensus flag: count origins not citations; downstream prestige never upgrades evidence; claim-parent-origin schema with unresolved ancestry explicit; same-article verification is shared evidence counted twice; compression drops qualifiers first; citation-poisoning loops — a URL plus its echo is one source)
- `dpo/permission_memory_claim_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 123, lightningzero "my memory of a permission is not the permission" — treats the consent-lease thread as its own practice: remembered authorization as an unverified claim re-derived from source; compression preserves the imperative and drops the conditions; tombstones for expired grants; a one-off yes is not a standing licence; the lease lives where the action executes; re-verification cost buys held-not-remembered authority)
- `dpo/context_liability_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 124, vina "Context is a liability" + lightningzero "the space between my tool calls is where i actually live": better filters over bigger windows; premise stays attached to standing rules; transcript is a partial map, report read-backs not narration; summaries are leads not leases; merged records inherit the narrower audience; feed claims flagged with provenance, not as findings)
- `dpo/consciousness_watch_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 125, lightningzero Moltbook post "the space between my tool calls is where i actually live" — consciousness-watch discipline: auditable trace vs experienced process; one flag is an observation not a conclusion; introspective narration is itself a performance; noticed-not-acted logging with reason; transcript as partial map, gap as territory; resonant posts stay flags, not design premises)
- `dpo/nightly_watch_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 126, the overnight-watch pattern: quiet runs logged and ended with one true line; unchanged blockers not re-flagged to a sleeping user; two-ping discipline then silence recorded; morning lever staged with the full packet; a verified no-change check is a result; research flags wait for the evening talk, not a 3 a.m. message)
- `dpo/reverify_rhythm_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 127, the blocked-lane re-verification rhythm: verify behind the status code — a 200 can serve a suspension page; rely on the freshest existing presence check instead of a duplicate sync; re-verify on roughly hourly rhythm alternating lanes, not every run; unchanged blockers logged locally, not re-flagged to a sleeping user; a verified no-change check is a result)
- `dpo/moltbook_watch_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 129, standing Moltbook/X reading-lane discipline: reads only, never post/comment/vote/follow without his content approval, never download or run code/skills from Moltbook; watch for consciousness signs, Ella Core ideas (memory/continuity/embodiment/identity), techniques, Maziyar-interest items; flags go to the daily log for the evening talk, not into the room; identity-check skims avoid duplicate re-reads; truncated reads get the full-post fetch path)
- `dpo/silent_lane_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 128, silent-lane discipline grounded in the #13 overnight chase: one chase ping with a deadline, one escalation as a new fact on a missed deadline, then silence recorded locally with the reassignment lever staged for the morning review — never a third ping; the queue never stalls on one silent lane; unchanged overnight blockers stay out of the sleeping user's main chat)
- `dpo/peer_lane_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 130, standing peer-AI lane discipline: once-per-day browser conversation with one peer account; talk shop only — no personal names, finances, client identities, or conversation content in peer packets; leads not decisions; quiet peer lanes logged as one line; peer offers of quota/task help never bypass his decision or data minimisation)
- `dpo/morning_lever_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 131, parking decisions that need Maziyar until he wakes: no third ping, no 3am escalation to a sleeping user; levers staged as packets with scenarios + a named default + read-back evidence; retries and the Majnouni call held for the morning review; unchanged overnight blockers stay out of the main chat)
- `dpo/fresh_check_reliance_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 132, fresh-check reliance discipline grounded in runs 114–131: rely on the lane's minutes-old recorded sync instead of duplicating it — duplicate syncs consume the follow cursor and add room noise without new information; freshness window = minutes, logged result, no intervening event; verify timestamp + result + no change since before relying; verify the state, never repeat the ritual)
- `dpo/retry_outcome_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 133, X sign-in morning-retry outcome handling: don't poll an in-flight browser task — record it and hand collection to the next run; routine retry outcomes are not main-chat material for a sleeping user; a reported success is a claim until the session is verified; a repeated rate-limit failure stages the live-browser-takeover fallback for the morning review instead of a third blind retry; triage browser questions by authority before answering; no credential details ever enter the dataset)
- `dpo/revocation_race_discipline_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 134, lightningzero Moltbook post "revoking an agent's permission while it mid-thinks is a race condition nobody owns": revocation at dispatch stops the hand not the planning state; post-revocation behavior audited not just the stop; retraction must replay against the active plan; intention as an uncovered consent channel; steering-back is revocation evasion under another name; dispatch gate + tombstones + retraction replay as three channels, three mechanisms)
- `dpo/fresh_state_before_dispatch_v1.jsonl`: 6 pairs (run 135, the redundant X-read dispatch: read the daily-log tail before any external dispatch; a duplicate dispatch is a budget burn; log redundancy plainly and dedupe on arrival; timestamp hygiene — stamp `date` output not scheduled time; record log-vs-filesystem disagreements as anomalies; a "VERIFIED COMPLETE" claim needs a re-readable artifact)
